<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

by check mark whether the registrant is a large accelerated filer, an accelerated filer, a non-accelerated filer, smaller reporting company,
or an emerging growth company. See the definitions of &#8220;large accelerated filer,&#8221; &#8220;accelerated filer,&#8221; &#8220;smaller
reporting company,&#8221; and &#8220;emerging growth company&#8221; in Rule 12b-2 of the Exchange Act.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>TEVOGEN
BIO HOLDINGS INC.</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTES
TO THE UNAUDITED CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000468" name="us-gaap:BusinessDescriptionAndBasisOfPresentationTextBlock"><p id="xdx_801_eus-gaap--BusinessDescriptionAndBasisOfPresentationTextBlock_zLTkIhZ9NNS1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
1. <span id="xdx_82A_zKSgtdXAHgkg">NATURE OF BUSINESS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Tevogen
Bio Holdings Inc., a Delaware corporation (the &#8220;Company&#8221;), is a clinical-stage specialty immunotherapy company harnessing
the power of CD8+ cytotoxic T lymphocytes (&#8220;CD8+ CTLs&#8221;) to develop off-the-shelf, precision T cell therapies for the treatment
of infectious diseases, cancers, and other disorders. The Company&#8217;s precision T cell technology, ExacTcell<sup>TM</sup>, is a set
of processes and methodologies to develop, enrich, and expand single human leukocyte antigen-restricted CTL therapies with proactively
selected, precisely defined targets. The Company has completed a Phase 1 proof-of-concept trial for the first clinical product of ExacTcell,
TVGN 489, for the treatment of ambulatory, high-risk adult COVID-19 patients, and has other product candidates in its pipeline.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
addition, through the Company&#8217;s Tevogen.AI artificial intelligence (&#8220;AI&#8221;) initiative, it is focused on harnessing the
potential of AI to expedite drug development, optimize laboratory processes and clinical trials, unravel complex biological data, improve
patient outcomes, and pass on related savings to patients.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Reverse
Stock Split</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective
March 6, 2026, the Company effected a reverse stock split at a ratio of <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--StockholdersEquityReverseStockSplit_c20260306__20260306_zD8ZA15Zuaya" title="Reverse stock split ratio"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-03-062026-03-06" id="Fact000470" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityReverseStockSplit">1-for-50</ix:nonNumeric></span> shares of its common stock (the &#8220;Reverse Stock
Split&#8221;). As a result, every fifty shares of the Company&#8217;s issued and outstanding common stock were automatically combined
into one share. The Reverse Stock Split affected all stockholders uniformly and did not alter any stockholder&#8217;s percentage ownership
interest in the Company.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">No
fractional shares were issued as a result of the Reverse Stock Split and the split did not impact the par value of the Company&#8217;s
common stock. Any fractional shares that would otherwise have resulted from the Reverse Stock Split were rounded down to the next whole
share.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000472" name="TVGN:DevelopmentStageRisksAndLiquidityTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80C_ecustom--DevelopmentStageRisksAndLiquidityTextBlock_zug4YRVzWl2e" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
2. <span id="xdx_827_zc3PukL91YIl">DEVELOPMENT-STAGE RISKS AND LIQUIDITY</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has generally incurred losses and negative cash flows from operations since inception. The Company anticipates incurring additional
losses until such time, if ever, that it can generate significant sales from its product candidates currently in development. On July
3, 2025, the Company entered into a Sales Agreement (the &#8220;Sales Agreement&#8221;) with A.G.P./Alliance Global Partners (the &#8220;Agent&#8221;),
pursuant to which the Company may issue and sell from time to time up to $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20250703__20250703__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SalesAgreementMember_zcZEWHe352l8" title="Value of shares of common stock"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-07-032025-07-03_custom_SalesAgreementMember" id="Fact000474" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">50,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of shares of common stock through the Agent as the
Company&#8217;s sales agent by any method that is deemed to be an &#8220;at-the-market&#8221; equity offering as defined in Rule 415
promulgated under the Securities Act of 1933, as amended. Management believes that cash of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--Cash_iI_c20260331_zGE9DliJuJzc"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000475" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">692,305</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March 31, 2026, net proceeds
of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfCommonStock_pn5n6_c20260401__20260515__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SalesAgreementMember_zzXnvvCYrh9e" title="Net proceeds from sale of common stock"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2026-04-012026-05-15_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember_custom_SalesAgreementMember" id="Fact000477" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million received from sales of common stock under the Sales Agreement subsequent to March 31, 2026, and net proceeds of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20260511__20260511__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--WarrantMember_zgsrCJ9oldvg" title="Aggregate purchase price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2026-05-112026-05-11_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember_us-gaap_WarrantMember" id="Fact000479" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
received from the sale of prefunded common stock purchase warrants in May 2026 (as described in Note 11), combined with the amounts available
under the Loan Agreement (as defined in Note 5) entered into in June 2024 and the remaining commitment for a $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--PurchaseCommitmentRemainingMinimumAmountCommitted_iI_c20260331_zNz0kGGIgcl8" title="Remaining commitment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PurchaseCommitmentRemainingMinimumAmountCommitted" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000481" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">7,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> grant from KRHP
LLC, a New Jersey limited liability company (&#8220;KRHP&#8221;), will allow the Company to have adequate cash and financial resources
to operate for at least the next 12 months from the date of issuance of these unaudited consolidated financial statements. The Company
does not plan to initiate a clinical trial until additional funding is received.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Management
regularly evaluates different strategies to obtain funding for operations for subsequent periods. These strategies may include but are
not limited to private placements of securities, licensing and/or marketing arrangements, partnerships with other pharmaceutical or biotechnology
companies, and public offerings of securities. The Company may not be able to obtain financing on acceptable terms and the Company may
not be able to enter into strategic alliances or other arrangements on favorable terms. The terms of any financing may adversely affect
the holdings or the rights of the Company&#8217;s stockholders. If the Company is unable to obtain sufficient funding, the Company could
be required to delay, reduce or eliminate research and development programs, product portfolio expansion, or future commercialization
efforts, which could adversely affect its business prospects.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Operations
since inception have consisted primarily of organizing the Company, securing financing, developing licensed technologies, performing
research, conducting pre-clinical studies and a clinical trial, and pursuing and completing the business combination pursuant to that
certain Agreement and Plan of Merger, dated June 28, 2023 (the &#8220;Merger Agreement&#8221;), by and among Semper Paratus Acquisition
Corporation, a Cayman Islands exempted company (&#8220;Semper Paratus&#8221;), Semper Merger Sub, Inc., a Delaware corporation and a
wholly owned subsidiary of Semper Paratus (&#8220;Merger Sub&#8221;), SSVK Associates, LLC, a Delaware limited liability company, in
its capacity as purchaser representative, Tevogen Bio Inc (n/k/a Tevogen Bio Inc.), a Delaware corporation (&#8220;Tevogen Bio&#8221;),
and Dr. Ryan Saadi, in his capacity as seller representative, pursuant to which Merger Sub merged with and into Tevogen Bio (the &#8220;Merger&#8221;),
with Tevogen Bio being the surviving company and a wholly owned subsidiary of Semper Paratus (the &#8220;Business Combination&#8221;).
The Company is subject to risks associated with any specialty biotechnology company that requires considerable expenditures for research
and development. The Company&#8217;s research and development projects may not be successful, products developed may not obtain necessary
regulatory approval, and any approved product may not be commercially viable. In addition, the Company operates in an environment of
rapid technological change and is largely dependent on the services of its employees and consultants.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000483" name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_808_eus-gaap--SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_z0OzBDup8HJ8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
3. <span id="xdx_822_zh1UNqrRq1Y4">SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
summary of significant accounting policies in Note 3 to the Company&#8217;s audited consolidated financial statements included in the
Annual Report on Form 10-K filed with the SEC on March 31, 2026 (the &#8220;Annual Report&#8221;) have not materially changed, except
as reflected in the following:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000485" name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84F_eus-gaap--BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zi6XVCIYbOa2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_867_zpnE45lRSsBi">Basis
of Presentation</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">These
unaudited consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance with U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (&#8220;GAAP&#8221;).
Any reference in these notes to applicable guidance is meant to refer to GAAP as found in the Accounting Standards Codification (&#8220;ASC&#8221;)
and Accounting Standards Updates (&#8220;ASU&#8221;) of the Financial Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;FASB&#8221;). In the opinion
of management, all adjustments considered necessary for a fair statement of the financial position and results of operations of the Company
have been included.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000487" name="us-gaap:FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_841_eus-gaap--FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock_z1a0NpIPhlAj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_860_ziIYzzAVh8n6">Fair
Value Measurements</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Certain
assets and liabilities are carried at fair value under GAAP. Fair value is defined as the price that would be received for an asset or
paid to transfer a liability (exit price) in the principal or most advantageous market for the asset or liability in an orderly transaction
between market participants on the measurement date. The Company utilizes valuation techniques that maximize the use of observable inputs
and minimize the use of unobservable inputs to the extent possible. When considering market participant assumptions in fair value measurements,
the following fair value hierarchy distinguishes between observable and unobservable inputs, which are categorized in one of the following
levels:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse">
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.65in; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 1</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Unadjusted quoted prices
    in active markets for identical assets or liabilities;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 2</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Observable inputs other
    than Level 1 prices, such as quoted prices for similar, but not identical, assets or liabilities in active markets; quoted prices
    for identical or similar assets or liabilities in markets that are not active; or other inputs that are observable or can be corroborated
    by observable market data;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 3</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Unobservable inputs in
    which there is little or no market data available and which require the Company to develop its own assumptions that market participants
    would use in pricing an asset or liability.</span></td></tr>
  </table>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Financial
instruments recognized at historical amounts in the balance sheets consist of accounts payable and notes payable. The Company believes
that the carrying value of accounts payable and notes payable approximates their fair values due to the short-term nature of these instruments.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify">T<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">here
were no transfers between levels during the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A1_zq5iAE4lW1U6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company used a Monte Carlo simulation (&#8220;MCS&#8221;) valuation methodology to determine the fair value of the freestanding $<span id="xdx_905_ecustom--FreeStandingPurchaseOptions_c20240101__20241231_zeDEBt5eQ7t9" title="Free standing purchase options"><ix:nonFraction name="TVGN:FreeStandingPurchaseOptions" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31" id="Fact000519" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">14,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> purchase option and remaining embedded
$<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--EmbeddedOptions_c20240101__20241231_zhglNtbWMX68" title="Embedded options"><ix:nonFraction name="TVGN:EmbeddedOptions" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31" id="Fact000521" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">14,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> purchase option associated with the Loan Agreement as of December 31, 2024. The MCS methodology simulates the Company&#8217;s
future stock price to estimate if and when the Trailing VWAP (as defined below) will reach $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--SharePrice_iI_uUSDPShares_c20260331_ztUsquKrIwEd" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000523" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">500.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per share (as adjusted for the March
2026 stock split), and discounts the resulting payoff back to each valuation date using a present value factor. Significant assumptions
used in determining the fair value of these options include volatilities of <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput_iI_pid_uPure_c20260331__us-gaap--MeasurementInputTypeAxis__us-gaap--MeasurementInputOptionVolatilityMember_ztDo12DofxEa"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_MeasurementInputOptionVolatilityMember" id="Fact000524" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Pure">78.98</ix:nonFraction></span> % and <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput_iI_pid_uPure_c20250331__us-gaap--MeasurementInputTypeAxis__us-gaap--MeasurementInputOptionVolatilityMember_zcNKWw064xR4"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput" contextRef="AsOf2025-03-31_us-gaap_MeasurementInputOptionVolatilityMember" id="Fact000525" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Pure">78.0</ix:nonFraction></span>% and discount rates of <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput_iI_pid_uPure_c20260331__us-gaap--MeasurementInputTypeAxis__us-gaap--MeasurementInputDiscountRateMember_zq04Mp74myKc" title="Alternative investment measurement input"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_MeasurementInputDiscountRateMember" id="Fact000527" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Pure">3.7</ix:nonFraction></span>% and <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput_iI_pid_uPure_c20250331__us-gaap--MeasurementInputTypeAxis__us-gaap--MeasurementInputDiscountRateMember_zm4pNjWhPhXa" title="Alternative investment measurement input"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AlternativeInvestmentMeasurementInput" contextRef="AsOf2025-03-31_us-gaap_MeasurementInputDiscountRateMember" id="Fact000529" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Pure">3.9</ix:nonFraction></span>% for
the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively. At March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the MCS produced a fair value
of $0 relating to these freestanding and embedded options.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000531" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_844_eus-gaap--EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock_zDs4L5KnS5w6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_86E_zLcXv0osi6S2">Net
Loss Per Share</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company computes basic net loss per share by dividing net loss by the weighted-average common stock outstanding during the period. Given
the Company&#8217;s net loss, basic and diluted net loss per share for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025 are the same.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000533" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zRGss1DGJvX9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="xdx_866_zgANe80yXYC5">Recently
Issued Accounting Standards</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
November 2024, the FASB issued ASU No. 2024-03, <i>Income Statement (Topic 220): Reporting Comprehensive Income - Expense Disaggregation
Disclosures, Disaggregation of Income Statement Expenses</i>, which requires public companies to disclose, in interim and annual reporting
periods, additional information about certain expenses in the financial statements. The amendments in this pronouncement will be effective
for annual periods beginning after December 15, 2026, and interim reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2027. Early adoption
is permitted and is effective on either a prospective basis or retrospective basis. The Company is currently assessing the potential
impacts of adoption on its unaudited consolidated financial statements and related disclosures.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
December 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-11, <i>Interim Reporting (Topic 270): Narrow Scope Improvements</i>. This update clarifies the
applicability of interim reporting guidance and the form and content of interim financial statements. It also establishes a disclosure
principle requiring an entity to disclose material events and changes occurring since the end of the last annual reporting period. ASU
2025-11 is effective for the Company for interim periods within annual reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2027, with early
adoption permitted. The Company is assessing the impact of adopting this standard.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Loan
Agreement</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
June 2024, the Company entered into a Loan Agreement (the &#8220;Loan Agreement&#8221;) with The Patel Family, LLP (the &#8220;Patel
Family&#8221;), a related party of the Company, providing for an unsecured line of credit facility (the &#8220;Facility&#8221;) for term
loans of up to an initial total of $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityMaximumBorrowingCapacity_iI_c20240630__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--UnsecuredDebtMember_zIW4GrZPTTF3" title="Line of credit maximum borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityMaximumBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2024-06-30_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_us-gaap_UnsecuredDebtMember" id="Fact000550" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">36,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. As of December 31, 2025, the Company had drawn $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityCurrentBorrowingCapacity_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--UnsecuredDebtMember_zX0anegmgWvd" title="Line of credit borrowing capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityCurrentBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_us-gaap_UnsecuredDebtMember" id="Fact000552" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,400,000</ix:nonFraction></span> from the Facility, with maturity
dates ranging from July 2028 to June 2029. The Company drew $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--LineOfCredit_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--UnsecuredDebtMember__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__custom--FebruaryToMarchTwoThousandThirtyMember_z4DDij5Kby6b" title="Line of credit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCredit" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_us-gaap_UnsecuredDebtMember_custom_FebruaryToMarchTwoThousandThirtyMember" id="Fact000554" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> during the first quarter of 2026, with a maturity date ranging
from February to March 2030. As of March 31, 2026, the outstanding balance on the Loan Agreement was $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--LineOfCredit_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--UnsecuredDebtMember_zeUT4ZL0urD8" title="Line of credit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCredit" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_us-gaap_UnsecuredDebtMember" id="Fact000556" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">6,400,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. As of March 31, 2026,
$<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__us-gaap--UnsecuredDebtMember_zkjxLxgYbInj" title="Line of credit remaining capacity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LineOfCreditFacilityRemainingBorrowingCapacity" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_us-gaap_UnsecuredDebtMember" id="Fact000558" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">14,000,000</ix:nonFraction> </span>remained available for future financing.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Loan Agreement includes a purchase option whereby the Patel Family has the option to purchase up to $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross_c20240601__20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zd63Q6mbcdT5" title="Option to purchase common stock, value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross" contextRef="From2024-06-012024-06-30_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000560" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">14,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of shares of common stock
at a purchase price equal to <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardPurchasePriceOfCommonStockPercent_pid_dp_uPure_c20240601__20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zCg3YVnBbUzb" title="Percentage of price of common stock purchased"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardPurchasePriceOfCommonStockPercent" contextRef="From2024-06-012024-06-30_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000562" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">70</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the Trailing VWAP per share (the &#8220;$<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross_pn6n6_c20240601__20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_z89HTdQ0ZP3h" title="Option to purchase common stock, value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross" contextRef="From2024-06-012024-06-30_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000564" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million Purchase Option&#8221;). The $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross_pn6n6_c20240601__20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zNnG3HLS8ASg" title="Option to purchase common stock, value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross" contextRef="From2024-06-012024-06-30_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000566" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million Purchase
Option only becomes exercisable once Trailing VWAP reaches $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--SharesIssuedPricePerShare_iI_c20240630__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_zxu77HJUPL0j" title="Purchase, price per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-06-30_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember" id="Fact000568" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">500.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per share. The $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--DerivativeLiabilities_iI_pn6n6_c20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zFi2JC3GrFR4" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2024-06-30_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember" id="Fact000570" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million Purchase Option was determined to be a
freestanding derivative liability under ASC 815 and is carried at fair value, with changes in fair value recorded to change in fair value
of written call option derivatives liabilities within the unaudited consolidated statements of operations and unaudited consolidated

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Loan Agreement also includes a purchase option (the &#8220;Additional Amount Purchase Option&#8221;) that is identical to the $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross_pn6n6_c20240601__20240630__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__custom--AdditionalAmountPurchaseOptionMember_zltrglcImqm8" title="Option to purchase common stock, value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockGrantedDuringPeriodValueSharebasedCompensationGross" contextRef="From2024-06-012024-06-30_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_AdditionalAmountPurchaseOptionMember" id="Fact000572" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million
Purchase Option, except that the option is exercisable for an amount up to the then-remaining undrawn term loan amount under the Loan
Agreement at the time Trailing VWAP reaches $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--SharesIssuedPricePerShare_iI_c20240630__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__custom--AdditionalAmountPurchaseOptionMember_z225MnBBvrrc" title="Purchase, price per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-06-30_custom_LoanAgreementMember_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_AdditionalAmountPurchaseOptionMember" id="Fact000574" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">500.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per share. The Additional Amount Purchase Option was determined to be an embedded
derivative within the written loan commitment that requires bifurcation under ASC 815, and thus is carried at fair value with changes
in fair value recorded to change in fair value of written call option derivatives liabilities within the unaudited consolidated statements
of operations and unaudited consolidated statements of cash flows.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
$<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DerivativeLiabilities_iI_pn6n6_c20260331_zuTlPdQspch6" title="Derivative liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000576" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million Purchase Option and the Additional Amount Purchase Option are recorded to written call option derivative liabilities within
the unaudited consolidated balance sheet and have a fair value of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--DerivativeLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_c20260331_zS6VK3waWLgk" title="Derivative liabilities, current"><span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--DerivativeLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_c20251231_zfaxPFX3FXyg" title="Derivative liabilities, current"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000578" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000580" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Loan Agreement is a written loan commitment that is not eligible for the fair value option under ASC 825, <i>Financial Instruments</i>.
However, management elected the fair value option for all draws under this commitment, and therefore has expensed all issuance costs
associated with the Loan Agreement, which are comprised of the fair value of the <span id="xdx_907_ecustom--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesCommitmentSharesInConnectionWithUnsecuredEquityLineOfCreditFacility_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zNqzNideKVT9" title="Common stock fair value"><ix:nonFraction name="TVGN:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesCommitmentSharesInConnectionWithUnsecuredEquityLineOfCreditFacility" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000582" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">20,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock issued to the Patel Family
as well as the issuance date fair value of the $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--DerivativeLiabilities_iI_pn6n6_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--ThePatelFamilyLLPMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LoanAgreementMember_zRBW6ZTQRnqh"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DerivativeLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_ThePatelFamilyLLPMember_custom_LoanAgreementMember" id="Fact000583" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">14</ix:nonFraction></span> million Purchase Option and Additional Amount Purchase Option.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Notes
Payable</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
a result of the Merger, the Company assumed notes payable held by Polar Multi-Strategy Master Fund (&#8220;Polar&#8221;) for which the
proceeds were to be used for working capital purposes by Semper Paratus with an outstanding balance of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--NotesPayableCurrent_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--PolarMultiStrategyMasterFundMember_z45GHspXo4o2" title="notes payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayableCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_PolarMultiStrategyMasterFundMember" id="Fact000585" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,651,000</ix:nonFraction></span> on the Closing Date
and which remain outstanding at March 31, 2026. The notes payable do not accrue interest. The outstanding balance of the notes was required
to be repaid in full within five business days of the Merger, and the Company is therefore in default of its obligations at March 31,
2026. The notes&#8217; default provisions do not require the Company to transfer any shares or pay any amounts to Polar.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000587" name="us-gaap:DisclosureOfCompensationRelatedCostsShareBasedPaymentsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80A_eus-gaap--DisclosureOfCompensationRelatedCostsShareBasedPaymentsTextBlock_zaFHvHiRz499" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
6. <span id="xdx_828_zVYzD5RQlTr3">STOCK-BASED COMPENSATION</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the Closing, the Company adopted the Tevogen Bio Holdings Inc. 2024 Omnibus Incentive Plan (the &#8220;2024 Plan&#8221;)
and no longer grants awards pursuant to the 2020 Equity Incentive Plan (the &#8220;2020 Plan&#8221;). Each restricted stock unit (&#8220;RSU&#8221;)
award granted under the 2020 Plan that was outstanding and unvested as of the Closing Date was automatically canceled and converted into
an award under the 2024 Plan with respect to the common stock of the Company (the &#8220;Rollover RSUs&#8221;). Such Rollover RSUs remain
subject to the same terms and conditions as set forth under the applicable award agreement prior to the Closing.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
addition to covering the Rollover RSUs, under the 2024 Plan, as of December 31, 2024, the Company was authorized to grant awards up to
an aggregate <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized_iI_pid_c20241231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_zuoUZB27aH8" title="Compensation shares authorized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember" id="Fact000589" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">800,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock. During the year ended December 31, 2025, the number of shares authorized under the 2024
Plan was increased to <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized_iI_pid_c20251231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_zry58USNd2Cl" title="Compensation shares authorized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember" id="Fact000591" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">977,991</ix:nonFraction></span>. The 2024 Plan provides for the grant of options, stock appreciation rights, restricted common stock (&#8220;Restricted
Stock&#8221;), RSUs, and other equity-based awards. As of March 31, 2026, awards for <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant_iI_pid_c20260331__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_zDorWru6R1B4" title="Shares available to be granted"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember" id="Fact000593" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">189,398</ix:nonFraction></span> shares remained available to be granted
under the 2024 Plan.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has issued RSUs that are subject to either service-based vesting conditions or service-based and performance-based vesting conditions.
Compensation expense for service-based RSUs is recognized on a straight-line basis over the vesting period of the award. Compensation
expense for service-based and performance-based RSUs (&#8220;Performance-Based RSUs&#8221;) is recognized when the performance condition,
which is based on a liquidity event condition being satisfied, is deemed probable of achievement. The fair value of RSUs vested during
the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025 was $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zOQYfugLzmvd" title="Fair value of RSUs vested"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact000595" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">0.3</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1_pn5n6_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember_zH9pJLQx897l" title="Fair value of RSUs vested"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_us-gaap_RestrictedStockUnitsRSUMember" id="Fact000597" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.8</ix:nonFraction></span> million, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
June 27, 2025, the Company issued an aggregate of <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesRestrictedStockAwardGross_c20250627__20250627__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember_zEL2Jdfurcb7" title="Number of shares issued for RSUs"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesRestrictedStockAwardGross" contextRef="From2025-06-272025-06-27_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_srt_ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember" id="Fact000599" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">185,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of Restricted Stock under the 2024 Plan to the Company&#8217;s executive
officers, including a grant of <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodGross_c20250627__20250627__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember_zALNaoKFnTLa" title="Number of shares issued for granted"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodGross" contextRef="From2025-06-272025-06-27_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_srt_ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember" id="Fact000601" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">160,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of Restricted Stock to Dr. Saadi. The shares of Restricted Stock granted to Dr. Saadi will
vest in four equal annual installments beginning on June 27, 2032 and the shares of Restricted Stock granted to each other grantee will
vest in three equal annual installments beginning on June 27, 2030 (the &#8220;RSA Vesting Period&#8221;), subject in each case to the
applicable grantee&#8217;s continuous service with the Company through the vesting date, and provided that the shares will automatically
vest in full in the event of termination due to death or disability. Pursuant to the terms of these awards, the Company&#8217;s executive
officers are entitled to vote the Restricted Stock, but the shares may not be sold, assigned, transferred, pledged, hypothecated, or
otherwise encumbered, subject to automatic forfeit. The Company&#8217;s executive officers will automatically forfeit all unvested Restricted
Stock in the event they depart the Company for any reason, unless termination of their service triggers accelerated vesting pursuant
to the terms of the applicable award agreement or the 2024 Plan. The fair value per share for these awards was determined to be $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodWeightedAverageGrantDateFairValue_uUSDPShares_c20250627__20250627__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember_zCTlX2568j2g" title="Fair value per share for award"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodWeightedAverageGrantDateFairValue" contextRef="From2025-06-272025-06-27_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_srt_ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember" id="Fact000603" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">62</ix:nonFraction></span> per
share, equivalent to the Company&#8217;s stock price on the grant date, resulting in a total grant date fair value of $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1_c20250627__20250627__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember_zx1flhnQAThc" title="Grant date fair value of shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharebasedCompensationArrangementBySharebasedPaymentAwardOptionsVestedInPeriodFairValue1" contextRef="From2025-06-272025-06-27_custom_TwoThousandTwentyFourPlanMember_srt_ChiefExecutiveOfficerMember" id="Fact000605" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">11,470,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. In
accordance with ASC 718, the Company will recognize compensation expense on a straight-line basis from the grant date until the completion
of the RSA Vesting Period.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

7. <span id="xdx_822_z93xxF9WWwh8">STOCKHOLDERS&#8217; DEFICIT</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Common
Stock</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March 31, 2026, the Company had <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesIssued_iI_c20260331_z2dfSSH4QBAl" title="Common shares, shares issued"><span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_c20260331_zFTPu1Uzg79a" title="Common shares, shares outstanding"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000714" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000716" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">4,168,705</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> shares of common stock issued and outstanding. For accounting purposes related to earnings
per share, only shares that are fully vested are considered issued and outstanding.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Transactions
with Sponsor</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Pursuant
to the Merger Agreement, the Company incurred $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--SponsorFees_c20260101__20260331_zGIlZcbHXPOh" title="Sponsor advisory service fee"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SponsorFees" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000775" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> in fees to SSVK Associates, LLC (the &#8220;Sponsor&#8221;) for advisory services
(the &#8220;Sponsor Advisory Service Fee&#8221;). In connection with the Merger and thereafter, the Company and Sponsor agreed that $<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--SponsorFeesPayable_iI_c20260331_z8mUPXnprsdi" title="Sponsor advisory services fee payable"><ix:nonFraction name="TVGN:SponsorFeesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000777" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
of the Sponsor Advisory Service Fee is payable in cash, $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--OtherLiabilities_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember_ztgxvHmjjrE2" title="Sponsor fee payable offset"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherLiabilities" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_SponsorMember" id="Fact000779" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction></span> would be offset against amounts due from the Sponsor, and the remainder
of the Sponsor Advisory Service Fee was paid with the issuance of <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesIssuedForServices_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SponsorAdvisoryServicesFeeMember_zLoHzLMZWkqd" title="Number of shares issued for sponsor fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesIssuedForServices" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_SponsorAdvisoryServicesFeeMember" id="Fact000781" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">3,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock at Closing. The Sponsor
Advisory Service Fee payable in cash is presented on the unaudited consolidated balance sheets under the line item &#8220;Due to related
party.&#8221;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

January 2025, the Company received a grant of $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_pn5n6_c20250101__20250131__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--KRHPLLCMember_zdyRFEfDmz4i" title="Grant fair value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-01-31_custom_KRHPLLCMember" id="Fact000795" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">2.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million from KRHP to further the Company&#8217;s development of off-the-shelf, genetically
unmodified precision T cell therapeutics to treat infectious diseases and cancers. In August 2025, the Company received an additional
grant of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_pn5n6_c20250801__20250831__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--KRHPLLCMember_zIopcJCpqLp" title="Grant fair value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-08-012025-08-31_custom_KRHPLLCMember" id="Fact000797" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million from KRHP to advance Tevogen.AI. </ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000801" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80C_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_znx1lZmZtZ16" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
10. <span id="xdx_82A_zVzkdzzVLHze">SEGMENT REPORTING</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company operates in <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--NumberOfOperatingSegments_dc_uSegment_c20260101__20260331_zqGVy5tvAhdf" title="Number of operating segment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NumberOfOperatingSegments" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000803" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="INF" unitRef="Segment">one</ix:nonFraction></span> operating segment, and therefore one reportable segment, and is focused on the global discovery, development
and commercialization of proprietary therapeutics. The Company&#8217;s business activities are managed on a consolidated basis through
the development and potential commercialization of pharmaceutical products, which are aimed at the global market in the event that products
are successful in receiving regulatory approvals. The Company&#8217;s determination that it operates as a single operating segment is
consistent with the financial information regularly reviewed by the chief operating decision maker for purposes of evaluating performance,
allocating resources, setting incentive compensation targets, and planning and forecasting for future periods. The Company&#8217;s chief
operating decision maker is the Chief Executive Officer.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
accounting policies for the Company&#8217;s single operating segment are the same as those described in the summary of significant accounting
policies. The Company&#8217;s single operating segment incurs expenses from the development of TVGN 489, which is developed by the Company&#8217;s
research and development department, designed to target various disease indications. The Company has not yet generated revenue in its
operating history.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For
the segment, the chief operating decision maker uses net loss, which is reported on the unaudited consolidated statements of operations
as consolidated net loss, to allocate resources (including employees, property, and financial resources), predominantly during the annual
budget and forecasting process. The chief operating decision maker also uses consolidated net loss, along with non-financial inputs and
qualitative information, to evaluate the Company&#8217;s performance, establish compensation, monitor budget versus actual results, and
decide the level of investment in the Company&#8217;s various research activities. The measure of segment assets is reported on the unaudited
consolidated balance sheet as total consolidated assets.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000805" name="us-gaap:SubsequentEventsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_801_eus-gaap--SubsequentEventsTextBlock_z919ANjqIvf5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
May 11, 2026, the Company sold in a private placement to existing investor the Patel Family prefunded common stock purchase warrants
exercisable for <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights_iI_c20260511__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--WarrantMember_zmT9C5O115ud" title="Number of common stock purchase warrants shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" contextRef="AsOf2026-05-11_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember_us-gaap_WarrantMember" id="Fact000807" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">375,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock for an aggregate purchase price of approximately $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_pn6n6_c20260511__20260511__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--WarrantMember_zE2IyOCxkpL3" title="Aggregate purchase price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2026-05-112026-05-11_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember_us-gaap_WarrantMember" id="Fact000809" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million.</span></p>
